Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on mcmc structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 396. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 167 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_396(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 396. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^167(S, F) is trivial. QED.
